local function step(a1, a2)
    -- Line: 299
    -- upvalues: Bees (val), wanderOn (val), nextBloom (val), flyTo (val)
    -- types: a1: table, a2: number
    local v1
    local leg = a1.leg

    if a1.state == "perch" then
        if a1.perchUntil <= a2 then
            a1.wanderAround = a1.at
            a1.wanderLeft = math.random(Bees.WanderLegs.min, Bees.WanderLegs.max)

            wanderOn(a1, a2)
        end

        return
    end

    if a1.state == "hover" then
        a1.at = a1.hoverAt
            + Vector3.new(0, math.sin((a2 + a1.phase) * 4) * 0.08, 0)
        v1 = (a2 + a1.phase) * 0.8
        a1.heading = Vector3.new(math.cos(v1), 0, (math.sin(v1)))

        if a1.perchUntil <= a2 then
            if 0 < a1.wanderLeft then
                wanderOn(a1, a2)
                return
            end

            v1 = nextBloom(a1.at, a1.flower)

            if not v1 then
                a1.state = "hover"
                a1.hoverAt = a1.at
                a1.perchUntil = a2 + 1
                a1.wanderLeft = 0

                return
            end

            a1.flower = v1
            a1.state = "fly"

            flyTo(a1, v1, a2)
        end

        return
    end

    if not leg then
        return
    end

    v1 = math.clamp((a2 - leg.startedAt) / leg.seconds, 0, 1)
    local v2 = v1 * v1 * (3 - v1 * 2)
    local v3 = 1 - v2
    local v4 = leg.from * (v3 * v3 * v3) + leg.c1 * (v3 * 3 * v3 * v2)
        + leg.c2 * (v3 * 3 * v2 * v2)
        + leg.to * (v2 * v2 * v2)
    v3 = leg.to - leg.from
    v2 = Vector3.new(v3.X, 0, v3.Z)
    local Unit = if not (0.001 < v2.Magnitude) then
        Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
    else
        Vector3.new(-v2.Z, 0, v2.X).Unit
    v2 = math.sin(3.141592653589793 * v1)
    v2 = v4
        + Unit
            * ((math.sin(v1 * 3.141592653589793 * 5 + a1.phase)) * Bees.WobbleStuds
                * v2)
    v4 = v2 - a1.at

    if 0.001 < v4.Magnitude then
        a1.heading = v4.Unit
    end

    a1.at = v2

    if v1 >= 1 then
        a1.leg = nil

        if a1.state == "wander" then
            a1.wanderLeft = a1.wanderLeft - 1

            if math.random() < Bees.HoverChance then
                a1.state = "hover"
                a1.hoverAt = a1.at
                local HoverSeconds = Bees.HoverSeconds
                a1.perchUntil = a2
                    + (HoverSeconds.min
                        + math.random() * (HoverSeconds.max - HoverSeconds.min))

                return
            end

            if 0 < a1.wanderLeft then
                wanderOn(a1, a2)
                return
            end

            v4 = nextBloom(a1.at, a1.flower)

            if v4 then
                a1.flower = v4
                a1.state = "fly"

                flyTo(a1, v4, a2)

                return
            end

            a1.state = "hover"
            a1.hoverAt = a1.at
            a1.perchUntil = a2 + 1
            a1.wanderLeft = 0

            return
        end

        a1.state = "perch"
        local LandSeconds = Bees.LandSeconds
        a1.perchUntil = a2
            + (LandSeconds.min + math.random() * (LandSeconds.max - LandSeconds.min))
    end
end

local function draw(a1, a2, a3, a4)
    -- Line: 365
    -- upvalues: Bees (val)
    -- types: a1: table, a2: number, a3: table, a4: table
    local v1 = Vector3.new(a1.heading.X, if a1.state ~= "perch" then a1.heading.Y * 0.4 else 0, a1.heading.Z)

    if v1.Magnitude < 0.001 then
        v1 = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
    end

    local v2 = CFrame.lookAt(a1.at, a1.at + v1.Unit)
    v1 = if a1.state ~= "perch" then
        math.abs((math.sin((a2 + a1.phase) * Bees.WingBeatsPerSecond * 3.141592653589793)))
            * 0.75
            + 0.25
    else
        0.15
    local BodySize = Bees.BodySize
    local v3 = BodySize.x / 2 + Bees.WingSize.x / 2
    local v4 = BodySize.y / 2
    local parts = a1.parts

    table.insert(a4, parts[1])
    table.insert(a3, v2)
    table.insert(a4, parts[2])
    table.insert(a3, v2)
    table.insert(a4, parts[3])
    table.insert(a3, v2 * CFrame.new(-v3 * 0.8, v4, 0) * (CFrame.Angles(0, 0, v1)))
    table.insert(a4, parts[4])
    table.insert(a3, v2 * CFrame.new(v3 * 0.8, v4, 0) * (CFrame.Angles(0, 0, -v1)))
end

local function inTerrain(a1) -- Line: 78; types: a1: vector

    local u10 = (Region3.new(a1 - Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), a1 + Vector3.new(1, 1, 1))):ExpandToGrid(4)
    local success, err, v2 = pcall(function() -- Line: 80; upvalues: u10 (val)
        return workspace.Terrain:ReadVoxels(u10, 4)
    end)

    end

    local X = Size.X

        Y = Size.Y

            Z = Size.Z

local function shortTime(a1) -- Line: 294; types: a1: number
    local v1, v2, v3

    if a1 < 10 then
        v1 = math.floor(a1 * 10 + 0.5) / 10

        if v1 == math.floor(v1) then
            return (("%*s"):format((math.floor(v1))))
        end

        return (("%*s"):format(v1))
    end

    if a1 < 60 then
        return (("%*s"):format((math.floor(a1 + 0.5))))
    end

    if a1 < 3600 then
        v1 = math.floor(a1 + 0.5)
        v2 = v1 // 60
        v3 = v1 % 60

        if v3 == 0 then
            return (("%*m"):format(v2))
        end

        return (("%*m %*s"):format(v2, v3))
    end

    v1 = math.floor(a1 / 60 + 0.5)
    v2 = v1 // 60
    v3 = v1 % 60

    if v3 == 0 then
        return (("%*h"):format(v2))
    end

    return (("%*h %*m"):format(v2, v3))
end

local function upgradeEntries(a1) -- Line: 1013

    end

    end

        v1,
        function(a1, a2) -- Line: 1033

            end

end

local function closeIfOutOfRange()
    -- Line: 1611
    -- upvalues: u205 (ref), u151 (ref), u53 (val), LocalPlayer (val), Config (val)
    local v1 = u205

    if v1 and u151.Visible then
        if not v1.Parent then
            u53.close()
            return
        end

        local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not HumanoidRootPart then
            return
        end

        local Magnitude = (HumanoidRootPart.Position - v1.Position).Magnitude

        if Config.Player.InteractReachStuds + 8 < Magnitude then
            u53.close()
        end
    end
end

local function recipeLines(a1, a2)
    -- Line: 1633
    -- upvalues: Config (val), u149 (ref), LocalPlayer (val), Palette (val)
    -- types: a1: string, a2: string
    local v1, v2, v3, v4, v5
    local v6 = Config.Description.BeltTips[a1]

    if v6 then
        return v6
    end

    v6 = {}
    local v7 = nil
    local v8 = nil

    for i, j in Config.Recipe.All, v7, v8 do
        if table.find(j.stations, a2)
            and j.family == nil
            and j.workbenchTier <= u149
            and not Config.Recipe.isSecret(j.output)
            and Config.Release.mayCraft(LocalPlayer, j.output) then
            v4 = {}

            for k, n in j.inputs do
                table.insert(
                    v4,
                    {name = Palette.displayName(k), count = n}
                )
            end

            table.sort(
                v4,
                function(a1, a2) -- Line: 1651
                    if a1.count ~= a2.count then
                        return a2.count < a1.count
                    end

                    return a1.name < a2.name
                end
            )

            v5 = {}
            v2 = nil
            v3 = nil

            for m, i5 in v4, v2, v3 do
                table.insert(v5, if not (1 < i5.count) then i5.name else ("x%* %*"):format(i5.count, i5.name))
            end

            v1 = Palette.displayName(j.output)
            v2 = if not (1 < j.outputCount) then v1 else ("x%* %*"):format(j.outputCount, v1)

            table.insert(
                v6,
                {
                    key = ("%* %*"):format(if not v4[1] then "" else v4[1].name, v1),
                    text = ("%* = %*"):format(table.concat(v5, " + "), v2),
                }
            )
        end
    end

    table.sort(
        v6,
        function(a1, a2) -- Line: 1668
            return a1.key < a2.key
        end
    )

    local v9 = {}

    for i6, i7 in v6 do
        table.insert(v9, i7.text)
    end

    return v9
end

local function stamp(a1, a2, a3, a4)

                j.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                j.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic

local function playNamed(a1, a2) -- Line: 441; types: a1: table, a2: string
    local rig = a1.rig
    local v1 = rig and rig.tracks[a2]

    if v1 and #v1 ~= 0 then
        local v2 = v1[math.random(#v1)]

        if rig.held then
            if a2 == "draw" or a2 == "release" then
                rig.held:Stop(0.05)

                rig.held = nil
            end
        end

        if a2 ~= "draw" then
            v2:Play(0.08)
            return
        end

        local def = a1.def and a1.def.drawSeconds
        v2:Play(0.08, 1, if not def then 1 else if not (def > 0) then 1 else if not (0 < v2.Length) then 1 else v2.Length / def)

        rig.held = v2
        rig.heldSince = os.clock()
    end
end

local function holdDraw(a1, a2) -- Line: 470; types: a1: table, a2: table
    local held = a2.held

    if not held then
        return
    end

    local drawSeconds = a1.def and a1.def.drawSeconds or 1
    local v1 = os.clock() - (a2.heldSince or 0)

    if drawSeconds + 1.5 < v1 then
        held:Stop(0.2)

        a2.held = nil

        return
    end

    if held.Length <= 0 then
        return
    end

    local v2 = math.max(held.Length - 0.03, 0)

    if not held.IsPlaying then
        held:Play(0, 1, 0)

        held.TimePosition = v2

        return
    end

    if 0 < held.Speed and v2 <= held.TimePosition then
        held:AdjustSpeed(0)

        held.TimePosition = v2
    end
end

local function jellyScale(a1, a2) -- Line: 495; upvalues: Config (val); types: a1: table, a2: number
    local hopAt = a1.hopAt

    if a1.jelly and hopAt then
        local hopSecondsInAir = Config.Enemy.HubGuardians.hopSecondsInAir
        local v1 = a2 - hopAt

        if not (v1 < 0) and not (hopSecondsInAir <= 0) then
            if v1 < hopSecondsInAir then
                return math.sin((math.clamp(v1 / hopSecondsInAir, 0, 1)) * 3.141592653589793)
                    * 0.36
                    + 1
            end

            local v2 = v1 - hopSecondsInAir

            if v2 < 0.16 then
                return 1 - 0.52 * (1 - v2 / 0.16)
            end

            return 1
        end

        return 1
    end

    return 1
end

local function poseAt(a1, a2) -- Line: 516; types: a1: table, a2: number
    local v1, v2
    local snapshots = a1.snapshots
    local v3 = snapshots[1]

    if a2 <= v3.at then
        return v3.cframe
    end

    local v4 = #snapshots

    for i = 2, v4 do
        v3 = snapshots[i]

        if a2 <= v3.at then
            v1 = snapshots[i - 1]
            v2 = v3.at - v1.at

            return v1.cframe:Lerp(v3.cframe, if not (v2 > 0) then 1 else (a2 - v1.at) / v2)
        end
    end

    return snapshots[#snapshots].cframe
end

local function stretched(a1, a2, a3) -- Line: 535; types: a1: table, a2: number, a3: number
    local offset = a1.offset

    local function along(a1) -- Line: 537; upvalues: a3 (val), a2 (val); types: a1: vector
        return (math.sqrt((a1.X * a3) ^ 2 + (a1.Y * a2) ^ 2 + (a1.Z * a3) ^ 2))
    end

    local X = a1.size.X
    local RightVector = offset.RightVector
    local v1 = X
        * math.sqrt((RightVector.X * a3) ^ 2 + (RightVector.Y * a2) ^ 2 + (RightVector.Z * a3) ^ 2)
    local Y = a1.size.Y
    local UpVector = offset.UpVector
    local v2 = Y
        * math.sqrt((UpVector.X * a3) ^ 2 + (UpVector.Y * a2) ^ 2 + (UpVector.Z * a3) ^ 2)
    local Z_3 = a1.size.Z
    local v3 = -offset.LookVector

    return (Vector3.new(v1, v2, Z_3 * (math.sqrt((v3.X * a3) ^ 2 + (v3.Y * a2) ^ 2 + (v3.Z * a3) ^ 2))))
end

local function draw(a1, a2)
    -- Line: 550
    -- upvalues: poseAt (val), u48 (val), u49 (val), holdDraw (val), jellyScale (val), stretched (val)
    -- types: a1: table, a2: number
    local Position, copy_2, offset_2, size, v1
    local v2 = a2 - 0.1
    local v3 = poseAt(a1, v2)
    local rig = a1.rig

    if rig then
        table.insert(u48, rig.root)
        table.insert(u49, v3 * rig.offset)

        if a1.anchor then
            table.insert(u48, a1.anchor)
            table.insert(u49, v3)
        end

        holdDraw(a1, rig)

        v1 = poseAt(a1, v2 - 0.1)
        local Magnitude = Vector3.new(v3.X - v1.X, 0, v3.Z - v1.Z).Magnitude
        v1 = Magnitude > 0.08
        local walk = rig.tracks.walk and rig.tracks.walk[1]

        if v1 == rig.walking then
            if v1 and walk then
                rig.rate = rig.rate
                    + ((math.clamp(Magnitude / 0.1 / rig.pace, 0.05, 3)) - rig.rate) * 0.2
                walk:AdjustSpeed(rig.rate)
            end

            return
        end

        rig.walking = v1

        if not walk then
            return
        end

        if not v1 then
            walk:Stop(0.2)
            return
        end

        rig.rate = math.clamp(Magnitude / 0.1 / rig.pace, 0.05, 3)
        walk:Play(0.2, 1, rig.rate)

        return
    end

    local v4 = jellyScale(a1, v2)
    local at = a1.snapshots[#a1.snapshots].at

    if v4 == 1 and a1.lastScale == 1 then
        local posedAt = a1.posedAt

        if at + 0.1 < posedAt then
            return
        end
    end

    a1.posedAt = a2
    v2 = 1 / math.sqrt(v4)
    local v5 = v3 * CFrame.new(0, (v4 - 1) * a1.hitbox.Size.Y / 2, 0)
    v1 = true

    if v4 == 1 then
        v1 = a1.lastScale ~= 1
    end

    local v6 = nil
    local v7 = nil
    local v8 = a1

    for i, j in a1.pieces, v6, v7 do
        offset_2 = j.offset
        Position = offset_2.Position

        table.insert(u48, j.copy)
        table.insert(u49, v5 * CFrame.new(Position.X * v2, Position.Y * v4, Position.Z * v2) * offset_2.Rotation)

        if v1 then
            copy_2 = j.copy
            size = if v4 ~= 1 then stretched(j, v4, v2) else j.size
            copy_2.Size = size
        end
    end

    local anchor_2 = v8.anchor

    if anchor_2 then
        table.insert(u48, anchor_2)
        table.insert(u49, v3)
    end

    v8.lastScale = v4
end

local function flash(a1) -- Line: 619; upvalues: Workspace (val); types: a1: table

    if not folder then
        for i, j in a1.hitbox:GetChildren() do
            if j:IsA("Model") then
                folder = j
                break
            end
        end
    end

    if not folder then
        return
    end

    local CurrentCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

    if CurrentCamera
        and 300 < (CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position - a1.hitbox.Position).Magnitude then
        return
    end

    local Highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
    Highlight.Name = "HitFlash"
    Highlight.FillColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    Highlight.FillTransparency = 0.35
    Highlight.OutlineTransparency = 1
    Highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
    Highlight.Adornee = folder
    Highlight.Parent = folder

    task.delay(
        0.12,
        function() -- Line: 646; upvalues: Highlight (val)
            Highlight:Destroy()
        end
    )
end

function v1.start()
    -- Line: 889
    -- upvalues: CollectionService (val), track (val), untrack (val), RunService (val), Workspace (val), u32 (val)
    -- upvalues: Config (val), release (val), buildRig (val), draw (val), build (val), u48 (val), u49 (val)
    (CollectionService:GetInstanceAddedSignal("Enemy")):Connect(track)
    ;(CollectionService:GetInstanceRemovedSignal("Enemy")):Connect(untrack)

    for i, j in CollectionService:GetTagged("Enemy") do
        track(j)
    end

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        -- Line: 896
        -- upvalues: Workspace (upval), u32 (upval), untrack (upval), Config (upval), release (upval), buildRig (upval)
        -- upvalues: draw (upval), build (upval), u48 (upval), u49 (upval)
        local CFrame_2, drawStuds, snapshots, v1, v2
        local v3 = os.clock()
        local CurrentCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local Position = if not CurrentCamera then nil else CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position
        local v4 = nil
        local v5 = nil

        for i, j in u32, v4, v5 do
            if not i.Parent then
                untrack(i)
            elseif not j.dead then
                CFrame_2 = i.CFrame
                snapshots = j.snapshots
                v2 = snapshots[#snapshots]
                drawStuds = j.def and j.def.drawStuds
                    or Config.Appearance.MotionStuds

                if j.folder then
                    drawStuds = drawStuds * 1.15
                end

                v1 = true

                if Position ~= nil then
                    v1 = (CFrame_2.Position - Position).Magnitude <= drawStuds
                end

                if v1 then
                    if CFrame_2 ~= v2.cframe then
                        if 0.1 < v3 - v2.at then
                            table.insert(
                                snapshots,
                                {at = v3 - 0.016666666666666666, cframe = v2.cframe}
                            )
                        end

                        table.insert(
                            snapshots,
                            {at = v3, cframe = CFrame_2}
                        )

                        if #snapshots > 12 then
                            table.remove(snapshots, 1)
                        end
                    end

                    if j.folder and j.dirty then
                        release(j)
                    end

                    j.dirty = false

                    if j.folder then
                        draw(j, v3)
                    elseif j.rigged then
                        buildRig(j)

                        if j.folder then
                            draw(j, v3)
                        end
                    elseif not (#snapshots < 2) then
                        build(j)

                        if j.folder then
                            draw(j, v3)
                        end
                    end
                else
                    if CFrame_2 ~= v2.cframe then
                        if #snapshots > 1 then
                            table.clear(snapshots)

                            snapshots[1] = v2
                        end

                        v2.cframe = CFrame_2
                        v2.at = v3
                    end

                    if j.folder then
                        release(j)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if #u48 > 0 then
            Workspace:BulkMoveTo(u48, u49, Enum.BulkMoveMode.FireCFrameChanged)
            table.clear(u48)
            table.clear(u49)
        end
    end)
end

function v1.playOn(a1, a2, a3) -- Line: 981; upvalues: u32 (val); types: a1: userdata, a2: string, a3: boolean
    local v1 = u32[a1]
    local rig = v1

    if rig then
        rig = v1.rig
    end

    if not rig then
        return nil, "it is not drawn as a rig here: its model has no rig, or it is too far away"
    end

    local Action = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
    local Animation = Instance.new("Animation")
    Animation.AnimationId = a2
    local success, err = pcall(function() -- Line: 314; upvalues: rig (val), Animation (val)
        return rig.animator:LoadAnimation(Animation)
    end)

    if not success then
        v1 = nil
    elseif err then
        err.Looped = a3
        err.Priority = Action
        v1 = err
    else
        v1 = nil
    end

    if not v1 then
        return nil, "that animation would not load"
    end

    v1:Play(0.1)

    return v1, nil
end

local function promptInvite(a1)
    -- Line: 120
    -- upvalues: LocalPlayer (val), SocialService (val), HudController (val)
    -- types: a1: number?
    task.spawn(function()
        -- Line: 121
        -- upvalues: a1 (val), LocalPlayer (upval), SocialService (upval), HudController (upval)
        local ExperienceInviteOptions = Instance.new("ExperienceInviteOptions")

        if a1 then
            ExperienceInviteOptions.InviteUser = a1
        end

        ExperienceInviteOptions.LaunchData = ("inviter=%*"):format(LocalPlayer.UserId)
        local success, err = pcall(function()
            -- Line: 129
            -- upvalues: SocialService (upval), LocalPlayer (upval), a1 (upval)
            return SocialService:CanSendGameInviteAsync(LocalPlayer, a1)
        end)

        if success and err == false then
            HudController.showAlert("Your account can't send invites (check Roblox privacy settings)")
            return
        end

        if not pcall(function()
            -- Line: 136
            -- upvalues: SocialService (upval), LocalPlayer (upval), ExperienceInviteOptions (val)
            SocialService:PromptGameInvite(LocalPlayer, ExperienceInviteOptions)
        end) then
            HudController.showAlert("Couldn't open the invite panel")
        end
    end)
end

local function ago(a1) -- Line: 113; types: a1: number
    local v1 = math.max(0, (os.time()) - a1)

    if v1 < 60 then
        return "just now"
    end

    local v2 = math.floor(v1 / 60)

    if v2 < 60 then
        return (("%*m ago"):format(v2))
    end

    v1 = math.floor(v2 / 60)

    if v1 < 24 then
        return (("%*h ago"):format(v1))
    end

    v2 = math.floor(v1 / 24)

    if v2 < 7 then
        return (("%*d ago"):format(v2))
    end

    return (("%*w ago"):format((math.floor(v2 / 7))))
end
